  // micro-tile rows\nconstexpr size_t NR = 8;   // micro-tile cols (2 x 4 doubles)\nconstexpr size_t KC = 256; // k-block depth\n\ntypedef double v4d __attribute__((vector_size(32), aligned(8)));\n\n// Pack an mc x kc block of A (row-major, leading dim N) into MR-row panels:\n// for each panel, for each k, MR consecutive values (zero padded).\ninline void packA(const double* __restrict A, double* __restrict Ap, size_t N,\n                  size_t i0, size_t mc, size_t k0, size_t kc) {\n    for (size_t ip = 0; };\n    while (tiles(MC, NC) < 4 * nthreads && NC > 64) NC /= 2;\n    while (tiles(MC, NC) < 4 * nthreads && MC > 24) MC /= 2;\n    MC = roundUp(MC, MR);\n\n    const size_t nIB = (N + MC - 1) / MC;\n    const size_t nJB = (N + NC - 1) / NC;\n    const size_t kcMax = std::min(KC, N);\n\n    #pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        std::vector<double> Ap(MC * kcMax);\n        std::vector<double> Bp(roundUp(std::min(NC, N), NR) * kcMax);\n        double edge[MR * NR];\n\n        #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(dynamic, 1)\n        for (size_t jb = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {\n            m[i * N + j] = getPseudoRndValue(N, i, j);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Blocked, packed, register-tiled matrix multiplication parallelized with OpenMP.\n// Every C[i][j] is accumulated in increasing k order starting from 0.0, exactly\n// like the reference triple loop, so the result is numerically equivalent.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nnamespace {\n\nconstexpr size_t MR = 6;   // micro-tile rows\nconstexpr size_t NR = 8;   // micro-tile cols (2 x 4 doubles)\nconstexpr size_t KC = 256; // k-block depth\n\ntypedef double v4d __attribute__((vector_size(32), aligned(8)));\n\n// Pack an mc x kc block of A (row-major, leading dim N) into MR-row panels:\n// for each panel, for each k, MR consecutive values (zero padded).\ninline void packA(const double* __restrict A, double* __restrict Ap, size_t N,\n                  size_t i0, size_t mc, size_t k0, size_t kc) {\n    for (size_t ip = 0; ip < mc; ip += MR) {\n        const size_t mr = std::min(MR, mc - ip);\n        for (size_t k = 0; k < kc; ++k) {\n            size_t r = 0;\n            for (; r < mr; ++r) Ap[r] = A[(i0 + ip + r) * N + k0 + k];\n            for (; r < MR; ++r) {\n            acc[r][0] = v4d{0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0};\n            acc[r][1] = v4d{0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0};\n        }\n    } else {\n        for (size_t r = 0; r < MR; ++r) {\n            std::memcpy(&acc[r][0], C + r * ldc, sizeof(v4d));\n            std::memcpy(&acc[r][1], C + r * ldc + 4, sizeof(v4d));\n        }\n    }\n    for (size_t k = 0; }\n\n} // namespace\n\nvoid matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, \n                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {\n    if (N == 0) return;\n    const double* const a = A.data();\n    const double* const b = B.data();\n    double* const c = C.data();\n\n    // Choose tile sizes so there is enough parallel work for all threads.\n    const size_t nthreads = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads());\n    size_t MC = 96, NC = 512;\n    auto tiles = [N](size_t mc, size_t nc) { return ((N + mc - 1) / mc) * ((N + nc - 1) / nc); };\n    while (tiles(MC, NC) < 4 * nthreads && NC > 64) NC /= 2;\n    while (tiles(MC, NC) < 4 * nthreads && MC > 24) MC /= 2;\n    MC = roundUp(MC, MR);\n\n    const size_t nIB = (N + MC - 1) / MC;\n    const size_t nJB = (N + NC - 1) / NC;\n    const size_t kcMax = std::min(KC, N);\n\n    #pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        std::vector<double> Ap(MC * kcMax);\n        std::vector<double> Bp(roundUp(std::min(NC, N), NR) * kcMax);\n        double edge[MR * NR];\n\n        #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(dynamic, 1)\n        for (size_t jb = 0; jb < nJB; ++jb) {\n            for (size_t ib = 0; ib < nIB; ++ib) {\n                const size_t j0 = jb * NC, nc = std::min(NC, N - j0);\n                const size_t i0 = ib * MC, mc = std::min(MC, N - i0);\n                for (size_t k0 = 0; // cols of a packed B block (multiple of NR)\n\ntypedef double v4d __attribute__((vector_size(32), aligned(8)));\n\ninline size_t roundUp(size_t x, size_t m) { return (x + m - 1) / m * m; }\n\n// Pack an mc x kc block of A (row-major, leading dim N) into MR-row panels:\n// for each panel, for each k, MR consecutive values (zero padded).\ninline void packA(const double* __restrict A, double* __restrict Ap, size_t N,\n                  size_t i0, size_t mc, size_t k0, size_t kc) {\n    for (size_t ip = 0; ip < mc; ip += MR) {\n        const size_t mr = std::min(MR, mc - ip);\n        const double* src = A + (i0 + ip) * N + k0;\n        for (size_t k = 0; k < kc; ++k) {\n            size_t r = 0;\n            for (; r < mr; ++r) Ap[r] = src[r * N + k];\n            for (; r < MR; ++r) Ap[r] = 0.0;\n            Ap += MR;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Pack a kc x nc block of B into NR-column panels: for each panel, for each k,\n// NR consecutive values (zero padded).\ninline void packB(const double* __restrict B, double* __restrict Bp, size_t N,\n                  size_t k0, size_t kc, size_t j0, size_t nc) {\n    for (size_t jp = 0; jp < nc; jp += NR) {\n        const size_t nr = std::min(NR, nc - jp);\n        const double* src = B + k0 * N + j0 + jp;\n        for (size_t k = 0; k < kc; ++k) {\n            size_t q = 0;\n            for (; q < nr; ++q) Bp[q] = src[k * N + q];\n            for (; q < NR; ++q) Bp[q] = 0.0;\n            Bp += NR;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// C[MR x NR] (leading dim ldc) = (first ? 0 : C) + Ap * Bp, accumulated in k order.\ninline void microKernel(size_t kc, const double* __restrict Ap, const double* __restrict Bp,\n                        double* __restrict C, size_t ldc, bool first) {\n    v4d acc[MR][2];\n    if (first) {\n        for (size_t r = 0; r < MR; ++r) {\n            acc[r][0] = v4d{0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0};\n            acc[r][1] = v4d{0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0};\n        }\n    } else {\n        for (size_t r = 0; r < MR; ++r) {\n            std::memcpy(&acc[r][0], C + r * ldc, sizeof(v4d));\n            std::memcpy(&acc[r][1], C + r * ldc + 4, sizeof(v4d));\n        }\n    }\n    for (size_t k = 0; k < kc; ++k) {\n        v4d b0, b1;\n        std::memcpy(&b0, Bp, sizeof(v4d));\n        std::memcpy(&b1, Bp + 4, sizeof(v4d));\n        for (size_t r = 0; r < MR; ++r) {\n            const double a = Ap[r];\n            acc[r][0] += a * b0;\n            acc[r][1] += a * b1;\n        }\n        Ap += MR;\n        Bp += NR;\n    }\n    for (size_t r = 0; r < MR; ++r) {\n        std::memcpy(C + r * ldc, &acc[r][0], sizeof(v4d));\n        std::memcpy(C + r * ldc + 4, &acc[r][1], sizeof(v4d));\n    }\n}\n\n// Compute rows [i0, i0+m) x cols [j0, j0+n) of C using the packing buffers Ap, Bp.\nvoid multiplyBlock(const double* __restrict A, const double* __restrict B, double* __restrict C,\n                   size_t N, size_t i0, size_t m, size_t j0, size_t n,\n                   double* __restrict Ap, double* __restrict Bp) {\n    double edge[MR * NR];\n    for (size_t jc = j0; jc < j0 + n; jc += NC) {\n        const size_t nc = std::min(NC, j0 + n - jc);\n        for (size_t k0 = 0; k0 < N; k0 += KC) {\n            const size_t kc = std::min(KC, N - k0);\n            const bool first = (k0 == 0);\n            packB(B, Bp, N, k0, kc, jc, nc);\n            for (size_t ic = i0; ic < i0 + m; ic += MC) {\n                const size_t mc = std::min(MC, i0 + m - ic);\n                packA(A, Ap, N, ic, mc, k0, kc);\n                for (size_t jp = 0; jp < nc; jp += NR) {\n                    const size_t nr = std::min(NR, nc - jp);\n                    const double* bpan = Bp + jp * kc;\n                    for (size_t ip = 0; ip < mc; ip += MR) {\n                        const size_t mr = std::min(MR, mc - ip);\n                        const double* apan = Ap + ip * kc;\n                        double* ct = C + (ic + ip) * N + jc + jp;\n                        if (mr == MR && nr == NR) {\n                            microKernel(kc, apan, bpan, ct, N, first);\n                        } else {\n                            if (!first) {\n                                for (size_t r = 0; r < mr; ++r)\n                                    for (size_t q = 0; q < nr; ++q)\n                                        edge[r * NR + q] = ct[r * N + q];\n                            }\n                            microKernel(kc, apan, bpan, edge, NR, first);\n                            for (size_t r = 0; r < mr; ++r)\n                                for (size_t q = 0; q < nr; ++q)\n                                    ct[r * N + q] = edge[r * NR + q];\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Split `units` work units into `parts` near-equal contiguous ranges; return range p.\ninline void splitRange(size_t units, size_t parts, size_t p, size_t& begin, size_t& end) {\n    const size_t base = units / parts, rem = units % parts;\n    begin = p * base + std::min(p, rem);\n    end = begin + base + (p < rem ? 1 : 0);\n}\n\n} // namespace\n\nvoid matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, \n                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {\n    if (N == 0) return;\n    const double* const a = A.data();\n    const double* const b = B.data();\n    double* const c = C.data();\n\n    // Number of micro-tile rows / columns of C.\n    const size_t rowUnits = (N + MR - 1) / MR;\n    const size_t colUnits = (N + NR - 1) / NR;\n\n    // Use only as many threads as there is meaningful work for (>= ~4 MFLOP each).\n    const double flops = 2.0 * static_cast<double>(N) * static_cast<double>(N) * static_cast<double>(N);\n    size_t nthreads = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads());\n    nthreads = std::min(nthreads, std::max<size_t>(1, static_cast<size_t>(flops / 4e6)));\n    nthreads = std::min(nthreads, rowUnits * colUnits);\n\n    // Arrange the threads in a pr x pc grid, each owning one rectangle of C.\n    // Pick the factorization that minimizes redundant reads of A and B (pr + pc)\n    // and keeps rectangles as square as possible.\n    size_t pr = 1, pc = nthreads;\n    for (size_t r = 1; r <= nthreads; ++r) {\n        if (nthreads % r != 0) continue;\n        const size_t q = nthreads / r;\n        if (r > rowUnits || q > colUnits) continue;\n        const double cost = static_cast<double>(r) / static_cast<double>(rowUnits * MR) * NR +\n                            static_cast<double>(q) / static_cast<double>(colUnits * NR) * MR;\n        const double best = static_cast<double>(pr) / static_cast<double>(rowUnits * MR) * NR +\n                            static_cast<double>(pc) / static_cast<double>(colUnits * NR) * MR;\n        if (pc > colUnits || cost < best) { pr = r; pc = q; }\n    }\n\n    const size_t kcMax = std::min(KC, N);\n    const size_t apSize = roundUp(MC * kcMax, 8);\n    const size_t bpSize = roundUp(roundUp(std::min(NC, N), NR) * kcMax, 8);\n\n    #pragma omp parallel num_threads(static_cast<int>(pr * pc))\n    {\n        // Per-thread packing workspace: first touched by its owner (NUMA-local) and\n        // kept alive across calls to avoid repeated page faults / unmapping.\n        static thread_local std::vector<double> work;\n        if (work.size() < apSize + bpSize + 8) work.resize(apSize + bpSize + 8);\n        double* const ap = reinterpret_cast<double*>(\n            (reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(work.data()) + 63) & ~static_cast<uintptr_t>(63));\n        double* const bp = ap + apSize;\n\n        const size_t tid = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_thread_num());\n        const size_t nt = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_num_threads());\n        if (nt == pr * pc) {\n            size_t rb, re, cb, ce;\n            splitRange(rowUnits, pr, tid / pc, rb, re);\n            splitRange(colUnits, pc, tid % pc, cb, ce);\n            const size_t i0 = rb * MR, i1 = std::min(N, re * MR);\n            const size_t j0 = cb * NR, j1 = std::min(N, ce * NR);\n            if (i0 < i1 && j0 < j1)\n                multiplyBlock(a, b, c, N, i0, i1 - i0, j0, j1 - j0, ap, bp);\n        } else {\n            // Fewer threads than requested were granted: distribute row strips.\n            #pragma omp for schedule(dynamic, 1)\n            for (size_t ib = 0; ib < rowUnits; ib += MC / MR) {\n                const size_t i0 = ib * MR, i1 = std::min(N, (ib + MC / MR) * MR);\n                multiplyBlock(a, b, c, N, i0, i1 - i0, 0, N, ap, bp);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n'''\ns=s[:a]+new+s[b:]\nopen('matmul.cpp','w').write(s)\nEOF\ngrep -n \"#include\" matmul.cpp; q < nr; ++q) Bp[q] = src[k * N + q];\n            for (; q < NR; ++q) Bp[q] = 0.0;\n            Bp += NR;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// C[MR x NR] (leading dim ldc) = (first ? 0 : C) + Ap * Bp, accumulated in k order.\ninline void microKernel(size_t kc, const double* __restrict Ap, const double* __restrict Bp,\n                        double* __restrict C, size_t ldc, bool first) {\n    v4d acc[MR][2];\n    if (first) {\n        for (size_t r = 0; r < MR; ++r) {\n            acc[r][0] = v4d{0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0};\n            acc[r][1] = v4d{0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0};\n        }\n    } else {\n        for (size_t r = 0; r < MR; ++r) {\n            std::memcpy(&acc[r][0], C + r * ldc, sizeof(v4d));\n            std::memcpy(&acc[r][1], C + r * ldc + 4, sizeof(v4d));\n        }\n    }\n    for (size_t k = 0; k < kc; ++k) {\n        v4d b0, b1;\n        std::memcpy(&b0, Bp, sizeof(v4d));\n        std::memcpy(&b1, Bp + 4, sizeof(v4d));\n        for (size_t r = 0; r < MR; ++r) {\n            const double a = Ap[r];\n            acc[r][0] += a * b0;\n            acc[r][1] += a * b1;\n        }\n        Ap += MR;\n        Bp += NR;\n    }\n    for (size_t r = 0; r < MR; ++r) {\n        std::memcpy(C + r * ldc, &acc[r][0], sizeof(v4d));\n        std::memcpy(C + r * ldc + 4, &acc[r][1], sizeof(v4d));\n    }\n}\n\n// Compute rows [i0, i0+m) x cols [j0, j0+n) of C using the packing buffers Ap, Bp.\nvoid multiplyBlock(const double* __restrict A, const double* __restrict B, double* __restrict C,\n                   size_t N, size_t i0, size_t m, size_t j0, size_t n,\n                   double* __restrict Ap, double* __restrict Bp) {\n    double edge[MR * NR];\n    for (size_t jc = j0; jc < j0 + n; jc += NC) {\n        const size_t nc = std::min(NC, j0 + n - jc);\n        for (size_t k0 = 0; k0 < N; k0 += KC) {\n            const size_t kc = std::min(KC, N - k0);\n            const bool first = (k0 == 0);\n            packB(B, Bp, N, k0, kc, jc, nc);\n            for (size_t ic = i0; ic < i0 + m; ic += MC) {\n                const size_t mc = std::min(MC, i0 + m - ic);\n                packA(A, Ap, N, ic, mc, k0, kc);\n                for (size_t jp = 0; jp < nc; jp += NR) {\n                    const size_t nr = std::min(NR, nc - jp);\n                    const double* bpan = Bp + jp * kc;\n                    for (size_t ip = 0; ip < mc; ip += MR) {\n                        const size_t mr = std::min(MR, mc - ip);\n                        const double* apan = Ap + ip * kc;\n                        double* ct = C + (ic + ip) * N + jc + jp;\n                        if (mr == MR && nr == NR) {\n                            microKernel(kc, apan, bpan, ct, N, first);\n                        } else {\n                            if (!first) {\n                                for (size_t r = 0; r < mr; ++r)\n                                    for (size_t q = 0; q < nr; ++q)\n                                        edge[r * NR + q] = ct[r * N + q];\n                            }\n                            microKernel(kc, apan, bpan, edge, NR, first);\n                            for (size_t r = 0; r < mr; ++r)\n                                for (size_t q = 0; q < nr; ++q)\n                                    ct[r * N + q] = edge[r * NR + q];\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Split `units` work units into `parts` near-equal contiguous ranges; return range p.\ninline void splitRange(size_t units, size_t parts, size_t p, size_t& begin, size_t& end) {\n    const size_t base = units / parts, rem = units % parts;\n    begin = p * base + std::min(p, rem);\n    end = begin + base + (p < rem ? 1 : 0);\n}\n\n} // namespace\n\nvoid matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, \n                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {\n    if (N == 0) return;\n    const double* const a = A.data();\n    const double* const b = B.data();\n    double* const c = C.data();\n\n    // Number of micro-tile rows / columns of C.\n    const size_t rowUnits = (N + MR - 1) / MR;\n    const size_t colUnits = (N + NR - 1) / NR;\n\n    // Use only as many threads as there is meaningful work for (>= ~4 MFLOP each).\n    const double flops = 2.0 * static_cast<double>(N) * static_cast<double>(N) * static_cast<double>(N);\n    size_t nthreads = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads());\n    nthreads = std::min(nthreads, std::max<size_t>(1, static_cast<size_t>(flops / 4e6)));\n    nthreads = std::min(nthreads, rowUnits * colUnits);\n\n    // Arrange the threads in a pr x pc grid, each owning one rectangle of C.\n    // Pick the factorization that minimizes redundant reads of A and B (pr + pc)\n    // and keeps rectangles as square as possible.\n    size_t pr = 1, pc = nthreads;\n    for (size_t r = 1; r <= nthreads; ++r) {\n        if (nthreads % r != 0) continue;\n        const size_t q = nthreads / r;\n        if (r > rowUnits || q > colUnits) continue;\n        const double cost = static_cast<double>(r) / static_cast<double>(rowUnits * MR) * NR +\n                            static_cast<double>(q) / static_cast<double>(colUnits * NR) * MR;\n        const double best = static_cast<double>(pr) / static_cast<double>(rowUnits * MR) * NR +\n                            static_cast<double>(pc) / static_cast<double>(colUnits * NR) * MR;\n        if (pc > colUnits || cost < best) { pr = r; pc = q; }\n    }\n\n    const size_t kcMax = std::min(KC, N);\n    const size_t apSize = roundUp(MC * kcMax, 8);\n    const size_t bpSize = roundUp(roundUp(std::min(NC, N), NR) * kcMax, 8);\n\n    #pragma omp parallel num_threads(static_cast<int>(pr * pc))\n    {\n        // Per-thread packing workspace: first touched by its owner (NUMA-local) and\n        // kept alive across calls to avoid repeated page faults / unmapping.\n        static thread_local std::vector<double> work;\n        if (work.size() < apSize + bpSize + 8) work.resize(apSize + bpSize + 8);\n        double* const ap = reinterpret_cast<double*>(\n            (reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(work.data()) + 63) & ~static_cast<uintptr_t>(63));\n        double* const bp = ap + apSize;\n\n        const size_t tid = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_thread_num());\n        const size_t nt = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_num_threads());\n        if (nt == pr * pc) {\n            size_t rb, re, cb, ce;\n            splitRange(rowUnits, pr, tid / pc, rb, re);\n            splitRange(colUnits, pc, tid % pc, cb, ce);\n            const size_t i0 = rb * MR, i1 = std::min(N, re * MR);\n            const size_t j0 = cb * NR, j1 = std::min(N, ce * NR);\n            if (i0 < i1 && j0 < j1)\n                multiplyBlock(a, b, c, N, i0, i1 - i0, j0, j1 - j0, ap, bp);\n        } else {\n            // Fewer threads than requested were granted: distribute row strips.\n            #pragma omp for schedule(dynamic, 1)\n            for (size_t ib = 0; ib < rowUnits; ib += MC / MR) {\n                const size_t i0 = ib * MR, i1 = std::min(N, (ib + MC / MR) * MR);\n                multiplyBlock(a, b, c, N, i0, i1 - i0, 0, N, ap, bp);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n'''\ns=s[:a]+new+s[b:]\nopen('matmul.cpp','w').write(s)\nEOF\ngrep -n \"#include\" matmul.cpp; < CPU_SETSIZE; ++cpu)\n            if (CPU_ISSET(cpu, &mask)) cpus.push_back(cpu);\n\n        // Read (package, core) ids in parallel: hundreds of sysfs reads are slow serially.\n        const long n = static_cast<long>(cpus.size());\n        std::vector<long> pkg(n, -1), core(n, -1);\n        #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n        for (long i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n            for (int leaf = 0; leaf < 2; ++leaf) {\n                char path[128];\n                std::snprintf(path, sizeof(path), \"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu%d/topology/%s\",\n                              cpus[i], leaf == 0 ? \"physical_package_id\" : \"core_id\");\n                if (FILE* f = std::fopen(path, \"r\")) {\n                    long v;\n                    if (std::fscanf(f, \"%ld\", &v) == 1) (leaf == 0 ? pkg : core)[i] = v;\n                    std::fclose(f);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        if (std::find(pkg.begin(), pkg.end(), -1) != pkg.end() ||\n            std::find(core.begin(), core.end(), -1) != core.end()) {\n            t.order = cpus;\n            t.physicalCores = cpus.size();\n            return t;\n        }\n        std::set<std::pair<long, long>> seen;\n        std::vector<int> siblings;\n        for (long i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n            if (seen.insert({pkg[i], core[i]}).second) t.order.push_back(cpus[i]);\n            else siblings.push_back(cpus[i]);\n        }\n        t.physicalCores = t.order.size();\n        t.order.insert(t.order.end(), siblings.begin(), siblings.end());\n        return t;\n    }();\n    return topo;\n}\n\n// Persistent packing workspace shared by all threads (each uses its own slice).\n// Kept across calls so that neither allocation nor unmapping happens per call,\n// and backed by transparent huge pages where available to cut page-fault cost.\ndouble* workspace(size_t doubles) {\n    struct FreeDeleter { void operator()(void* p) const { std::free(p); } };\n    static std::unique_ptr<double, FreeDeleter> buf;\n    static size_t capacity = 0;\n    if (capacity < doubles) {\n        constexpr size_t kHuge = size_t(2) << 20;\n        const size_t bytes = roundUp(doubles * sizeof(double), kHuge);\n        buf.reset(static_cast<double*>(std::aligned_alloc(kHuge, bytes)));\n        if (!buf) throw std::bad_alloc();\n#ifdef MADV_HUGEPAGE\n        madvise(buf.get(), bytes, MADV_HUGEPAGE);\n#endif\n        capacity = bytes / sizeof(double);\n    }\n    return buf.get();\n}\n\n} // namespace\n\nvoid matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, \n                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {\n    if (N == 0) return;\n    const double* const a = A.data();\n    const double* const b = B.data();\n    double* const c = C.data();\n\n    // Number of micro-tile rows / columns of C.\n    const size_t rowUnits = (N + MR - 1) / MR;\n    const size_t colUnits = (N + NR - 1) / NR;\n\n    // Use only as many threads as there is meaningful work for (>= ~4 MFLOP each).\n    const double flops = 2.0 * static_cast<double>(N) * static_cast<double>(N) * static_cast<double>(N);\n    size_t nthreads = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads());\n    nthreads = std::min(nthreads, std::max<size_t>(1, static_cast<size_t>(flops / 4e6)));\n\n    // Unless the user configured thread placement, pin threads (physical cores first):\n    // the static decomposition relies on cache locality and suffers from migrations.\n    // If the thread count was not chosen explicitly either, use one thread per\n    // physical core, since SMT siblings only compete for the FMA units and caches.\n    const bool pinThreads = nthreads > 1 && omp_get_proc_bind() == omp_proc_bind_false;\n    const CpuTopology* topo = pinThreads ? &cpuTopology() : nullptr;\n    if (topo && topo->physicalCores > 0 && std::getenv(\"OMP_NUM_THREADS\") == nullptr)\n        nthreads = std::min(nthreads, topo->physicalCores);\n    nthreads = std::min(nthreads, rowUnits * colUnits);\n\n    // Arrange the work in a pr x pc grid of rectangles of C, one per thread.\n    // Pick the factorization that minimizes redundant reads of A and B, i.e.\n    // keeps the rectangles as square as possible.\n    auto gridCost = [&](size_t r, size_t q) {\n        return static_cast<double>(r) / static_cast<double>(colUnits * NR) +\n               static_cast<double>(q) / static_cast<double>(rowUnits * MR);\n    };\n    size_t pr = 1, pc = nthreads;\n    for (size_t r = 1; r <= nthreads; ++r) {\n        if (nthreads % r != 0) continue;\n        const size_t q = nthreads / r;\n        if (r > rowUnits || q > colUnits) continue;\n        if (pc > colUnits || gridCost(r, q) < gridCost(pr, pc)) { pr = r; pc = q; }\n    }\n    const size_t ntasks = pr * pc;\n\n    // Packing buffer sizes for the largest rectangle.\n    const size_t kcMax = std::min(KC, N);\n    const size_t maxRows = (rowUnits + pr - 1) / pr * MR;\n    const size_t maxCols = (colUnits + pc - 1) / pc * NR;\n    const size_t apSize = roundUp(std::min(MC, maxRows) * kcMax, 8);\n    const size_t bpSize = roundUp(std::min(NC, maxCols) * kcMax, 8);\n    const size_t perThread = apSize + bpSize;\n    double* const work = workspace(perThread * ntasks);\n\n    #pragma omp parallel num_threads(static_cast<int>(ntasks))\n    {\n        const size_t tid = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_thread_num());\n        cpu_set_t oldMask;\n        const bool pinned = topo && !topo->order.empty() &&\n                            pthread_getaffinity_np(pthread_self(), sizeof(oldMask), &oldMask) == 0;\n        if (pinned) {\n            cpu_set_t one;\n            CPU_ZERO(&one);\n            CPU_SET(topo->order[tid % topo->order.size()], &one);\n            pthread_setaffinity_np(pthread_self(), sizeof(one), &one);\n        }\n        double* const ap = work + tid * perThread;\n        double* const bp = ap + apSize;\n\n        // With a full team, schedule(static, 1) gives thread t exactly rectangle t.\n        #pragma omp for schedule(static, 1)\n        for (size_t t = 0; t < ntasks; ++t) {\n            size_t rb, re, cb, ce;\n            splitRange(rowUnits, pr, t / pc, rb, re);\n            splitRange(colUnits, pc, t % pc, cb, ce);\n            const size_t i0 = rb * MR, i1 = std::min(N, re * MR);\n            const size_t j0 = cb * NR, j1 = std::min(N, ce * NR);\n            if (i0 < i1 && j0 < j1)\n                multiplyBlock(a, b, c, N, i0, i1 - i0, j0, j1 - j0, ap, bp);\n        }\n\n        if (pinned) pthread_setaffinity_np(pthread_self(), sizeof(oldMask), &oldMask);\n    }\n}\n\n'''\ns=s[:a]+new+s[b:]\ns=s.replace('#include return;\n    const double* const a = A.data();\n    const double* const b = B.data();\n    double* const c = C.data();\n\n    // Number of micro-tile rows / columns of C.\n    const size_t rowUnits = (N + MR - 1) / MR;\n    const size_t colUnits = (N + NR - 1) / NR;\n\n    // Use only as many threads as there is meaningful work for (>= ~16 MFLOP each).\n    const double flops = 2.0 * static_cast<double>(N) * static_cast<double>(N) * static_cast<double>(N);\n    size_t nthreads = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads());\n    nthreads = std::min(nthreads, std::max<size_t>(1, static_cast<size_t>(flops / 16e6)));\n\n    // Unless the user configured thread placement, pin threads (physical cores first):\n    // the static decomposition relies on cache locality and suffers from migrations.\n    // If the thread count was not chosen explicitly either, use one thread per\n    // physical core, since SMT siblings only compete for the FMA units and caches.\n    // (Only for large problems: detecting the topology costs a few milliseconds.)\n    const bool pinThreads = nthreads > 1 && flops >= 1e9 && omp_get_proc_bind() == omp_proc_bind_false;\n    const CpuTopology* topo and B, i.e.\n    // keeps the rectangles as square as possible.\n    auto gridCost = [&](size_t r, size_t q) {\n        return static_cast<double>(r) / static_cast<double>(colUnits * NR) +\n               static_cast<double>(q) / static_cast<double>(rowUnits * MR);\n    };\n    size_t pr = 1, pc = nthreads;\n    for (size_t r = 1; r <= nthreads; ++r) {\n        if (nthreads % r != 0) continue;\n        const size_t q = nthreads / r;\n        if (r > rowUnits || q > colUnits) continue;\n        if (pc > colUnits || gridCost(r, q) < gridCost(pr, pc)) { pr = r; pc = q; }\n    }\n    const size_t maxCols = (colUnits + pc - 1) / pc * NR;\n    const size_t apSize = roundUp(std::min(MC, maxRows) * kcMax, 8);\n    const size_t bpSize = roundUp(std::min(NC, maxCols) * kcMax, 8);\n    const size_t perThread = apSize + bpSize;\n    double* const work = workspace(perThread * ntasks);\n\n    #pragma omp parallel num_threads(static_cast<int>(ntasks))\n    {\n        const size_t tid = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_thread_num());\n        cpu_set_t oldMask;\n        const bool pinned = topo && !topo->order.empty() &&\n                            pthread_getaffinity_np(pthread_self(), sizeof(oldMask), &oldMask) == 0;\n        if (pinned) {\n            cpu_set_t one;\n            CPU_ZERO(&one);\n            CPU_SET(topo->order[tid % topo->order.size()], &one);\n            pthread_setaffinity_np(pthread_self(), sizeof(one), &one);\n        }\n        double* const ap = work + tid * perThread;\n        double* const bp = ap + apSize;\n\n        // With a full team, schedule(static, 1) gives thread t exactly rectangle t.\n        #pragma omp for schedule(static, 1)\n        for (size_t t = 0; t < ntasks; ++t) {\n            size_t rb, re, cb, ce;\n            splitRange(rowUnits, pr, t / pc, rb, re);\n            splitRange(colUnits, pc, t % pc, cb, ce);\n            const size_t i0 = rb * MR, i1 = std::min(N, re * MR);\n            const size_t j0 = cb * NR, j1 = std::min(N, ce * NR);\n            if (i0 < i1 && j0 < j1)\n                multiplyBlock(a, b, c, N, i0, i1 - i0, j0, j1 - j0, ap, bp);\n        }\n\n        if (pinned) {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5-5","id":"msg_011CfjXvnSjLdUY2Qe8TrDfY","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"The matmul benchmark now runs in parallel with OpenMP. On this machine (2× EPYC 7763, 128 cores / 256 hardware threads) it reaches about 2.7 TFLOPS at N=8192. Only `matmul/matmul.cpp` and `matmul/CMakeLists.txt` were changed. The executable is still `matmul`, and there are no new dependencies.\n\n**Correctness.** `-v` validation passes for every size I tried, including small, odd and prime sizes and different thread counts. Comparing the full C matrix against the original code, the largest relative difference is about 8e-16. Results are not bit-identical to the original: the compiler builds the original loop with a mix of fused and separate multiply-adds, so the last bit can differ. My version adds the terms in the same order (k = 0 … N−1, starting from 0.0) and gives the same answer whatever the thread count.\n\n**Performance** (default settings):\n\n| N | Original (serial) | Now |\n|---|---|---|\n| 2048 | 99.7 s (0.17 GFLOPS) | 19 ms (904 GFLOPS) |\n| 4096 | – | 62 ms (2.2 TFLOPS) |\n| 8192 | – | 409 ms (2.7 TFLOPS) |\n\nScaling at N=4096 is nearly linear: 26.7 GFLOPS on 1 thread, 380 on 16, 1400 on 64, 2400 on 128.\n\n**What changed:**\n- **Matrix setup:** `initMatrix` now runs in parallel.\n- **Multiply kernel:** rewritten as a blocked loop. Pieces of A and B are copied into small aligned buffers, and a 6×8 inner block uses vector fused multiply-adds. One core reaches about 77% of its peak at the clock it actually runs.\n- **Work split:** C is divided into a grid of near-equal rectangles, one per thread. Each thread owns its part of C, so there are no locks or reductions. The grid shape is chosen to minimise re-reading A and B.\n- **Overheads removed:**\n  - Small problems use fewer threads.\n  - The scratch buffers are allocated once and kept for the program's lifetime. Allocating them per thread cost up to 48 ms per call with 256 threads.\n- **CMake:** adds `find_package(OpenMP REQUIRED)` and links `OpenMP::OpenMP_CXX`, so OpenMP is always used.\n\n**Decisions for you:**\n- **Thread pinning and count.** If `OMP_PROC_BIND`/`OMP_PLACES` aren't set and N is at least about 800, the code pins each thread to a CPU for the duration of the multiply, one per physical core first, then restores the original affinity. Without this, the OS moving threads between CPUs made N=4096 more than twice as slow. If `OMP_NUM_THREADS` isn't set either, it also uses only one thread per physical core (128 here), because two threads sharing a core were slower (N=4096: 121 ms vs 77 ms). Any of those environment variables you set yourself is respected as given. This uses Linux affinity calls and reads core layout from `/sys`, falling back to plain CPU order if that isn't available. If you'd rather leave placement entirely to OpenMP, it's a self-contained block that's easy to remove.\n- **Small-N noise.** Timings for small N (≤ ~1500) varied by several ms between runs. On this machine (2× EPYC 7763, 128 cores / 256 hardware threads) it reaches about 2.7 TFLOPS at N=8192. Only `matmul/matmul.cpp` and `matmul/CMakeLists.txt` were changed. The executable is still `matmul`, and there are no new dependencies.\n\n**Correctness.** `-v` validation passes for every size I tried, including small, odd and prime sizes and different thread counts. Comparing the full C matrix against the original code, the largest relative difference is about 8e-16. Results are not bit-identical to the original: the compiler builds the original loop with a mix of fused and separate multiply-adds, so the last bit can differ. My version adds the terms in the same order (k = 0 … N−1, starting from 0.0) and gives the same answer whatever the thread count.\n\n**Performance** (default settings):\n\n| N | Original (serial) | Now |\n|---|---|---|\n| 2048 | 99.7 s (0.17 GFLOPS) | 19 ms (904 GFLOPS) |\n| 4096 | – | 62 ms (2.2 TFLOPS) |\n| 8192 | – | 409 ms (2.7 TFLOPS) |\n\nScaling at N=4096 is nearly linear: 26.7 GFLOPS on 1 thread, 380 on 16, 1400 on 64, 2400 on 128.\n\n**What changed:**\n- **Matrix setup:** `initMatrix` now runs in parallel.\n- **Multiply kernel:** rewritten as a blocked loop. Pieces of A and B are copied into small aligned buffers, and a 6×8 inner block uses vector fused multiply-adds. One core reaches about 77% of its peak at the clock it actually runs.\n- **Work split:** C is divided into a grid of near-equal rectangles, one per thread. Each thread owns its part of C, so there are no locks or reductions. The grid shape is chosen to minimise re-reading A and B.\n- **Overheads removed:**\n  - Small problems use fewer threads.\n  - The scratch buffers are allocated once and kept for the program's lifetime. Allocating them per thread cost up to 48 ms per call with 256 threads.\n- **CMake:** adds `find_package(OpenMP REQUIRED)` and links `OpenMP::OpenMP_CXX`, so OpenMP is always used.\n\n**Decisions for you:**\n- **Thread pinning and count.** If `OMP_PROC_BIND`/`OMP_PLACES` aren't set and N is at least about 800, the code pins each thread to a CPU for the duration of the multiply, one per physical core first, then restores the original affinity. Without this, the OS moving threads between CPUs made N=4096 more than twice as slow. If `OMP_NUM_THREADS` isn't set either, it also uses only one thread per physical core (128 here), because two threads sharing a core were slower (N=4096: 121 ms vs 77 ms). Any of those environment variables you set yourself is respected as given. This uses Linux affinity calls and reads core layout from `/sys`, falling back to plain CPU order if that isn't available. If you'd rather leave placement entirely to OpenMP, it's a self-contained block that's easy to remove.\n- **Small-N noise.** Timings for small N (≤ ~1500) varied by several ms between runs.
